# Mesiri V1 Tracker — DPR for construction

> **The single list of V1 work.** Every session (Claude Code or human) checks this file
> before starting V1 work and updates it when claiming and finishing an item.
>
> - **What V1 is:** one feature, an automatic daily progress report (DPR) for construction
>   companies, on production-grade foundations (AI layer, tenancy, infra, superadmin,
>   dashboard). Surfaces: **web dashboard + WhatsApp assistant only**. Mobile app parked.
> - **Why and what was decided:** the architecture doc
>   ([Mesiri — Target Architecture](https://claude.ai/code/artifact/399eaa7f-c055-4e17-b3aa-a916670dd901)),
>   the [database map](https://claude.ai/code/artifact/80bacce6-0497-4a75-aa1b-08a5cd93c259)
>   and the [UI design](https://claude.ai/artifact/WW1bwDkePpTp8EWK2EswBu).
> - **Decisions:** read [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) first; never re-ask or contradict one.
> - **Process:** [CLAUDE.md](../../CLAUDE.md) still applies to every item: investigate,
>   plan, wait for approval, implement, test, push to `main`, report.

## How to use this file with several sessions at once

1. **Pull first.** `git pull origin main`, then read this file.
2. **Pick an item** whose status is `todo` and whose **Depends on** items are all `done`
   (or say `—`). Prefer `Must` over `Later`.
3. **Claim it before working.** Change its row to `claimed`, put your name/session in
   **Owner**, then commit and push *only this file* straight away:
   `git commit -m "tracker: claim BE-15"`. If the push is rejected, pull: if someone else
   claimed it first, pick another item.
4. **One item per session at a time.** Don't edit other people's rows, except to add a note.
5. **While working:** `in-progress`. If stuck on a decision or another item: `blocked`
   with the reason in **Notes**.
6. **When finished and pushed:** `done`, with the commit hash(es) in **Notes**. Commit
   messages for the work itself start with the item ID, e.g. `feat(dpr): BE-15 add site_days`.
7. **New work found?** Add a row at the end of the right section with the next free ID,
   status `todo`, and say so in your report. Don't renumber existing IDs.

**Status values:** `todo` · `claimed` · `in-progress` · `blocked` · `done` · `dropped`
**Size (rough, focused days):** S ≈ 1 · M ≈ 2–4 · L ≈ 5–10. Estimates, not commitments.

No migration. Between a project's cutoff and send deadline, each site whose day has no work items or no labour count gets one message to its engineer (else that day's reporters, else admins/PMs): what's missing, the deadline, **Site closed today** button. Replies are saved as usual; the day is refreshed within a tick (new version only if changed), so a filled gap turns it ready. Runs in the automation runner's tick; skips quiet hours like the DPR send. "1 of 1 site not reported" in the project DPR message (and the WhatsApp template line) now names a site with saved-but-unconfirmed updates and its count; reports are built and confirmed exactly as before. **Not verified:** the production logs of the 21:11/21:16 requests (so which of (1)-(3) was the live cause is unproven); live check pending (Ilan Pro sends "Send me today's report" after deploy). Slice 2 (Live, `pages/LivePage.tsx`): a draft's chip waits in amber ("⏳ … · Waiting for ✅ Save on WhatsApp · not on the report yet", "N waiting for Save" above the sheet); the tap plays it in ("Saved · Manpower" full size, then onto the report); ❌ Cancel strikes the chip out; saving or cancelling a manpower line is never shown as a correction. Slice 3 (stock): `fact_review.movement_counted_sql` leaves out ledger movements from a draft receipt/usage, and the incoming half of a transfer whose outgoing half is a draft; used by `get_stock_levels` (dashboard inventory and WhatsApp stock answers), `get_ledger` and the DPR's opening/closing stock. The over-stock hint while capturing still counts unsaved entries (`include_unsaved=True` in `material_entry.py` and `seeding.py`), so "10 came" then "8 used" before either Save is not flagged; write-time stock locks/checks (`get_available_stock`) are unchanged. A rejected draft still nets to zero (rejected + its reversal). Slice 4 (attendance, site log, issues): `workforce.COUNTED_LINE_SQL` counts a line once its report is saved, and an unsaved later report replaces nothing (Labour page, Today headcount, analytics, WhatsApp labour answers); Site log and Issues lists, analytics work/issue counts and WhatsApp "what work was done" answers leave out unsaved entries, while the agent's own picture of today still sees them (`include_unsaved=True` in `today_context.py`) so it never logs a thing twice. A day whose only entries are unsaved is not "missing work/labour" (`site_day.missing_sections`): the evening message says to keep them under Fix something. Unchanged on purpose: capture-time lookups (open work to continue, correction targets, today's labour list to replace), write-time stock checks, "has the site reported" signals. Verified also against a local Postgres 16: backend integration 367 passed (2 need Redis), assistant integration as before WA-77 (1 pre-existing failure); e2e tests now tap Save (`tests/integration/_tap_save.py`). |

## SS — Self-serve signup & trials

Anyone can message the Mesiri WhatsApp number (or sign up on the web) and get their own
company on a free trial; their team joins by link with the owner's approval. Decided
13 Oct 2026: **anyone can sign up**; **trial length and limits are superadmin settings, not
fixed** (default and per company); **conversion to paid is manual for now**, online payment
later. Every path must use the same company-creation step as the superadmin wizard.

## TM — AI teammate (reusable, `teammate/`)

Claude Code as a team member: listens to the team and customer WhatsApp groups, keeps an inbox any Claude Code session can search, later builds approved items and asks for sign-off in the group. Self-contained folder so other projects can reuse it.

| ID | Item | Size | Priority | Depends on | Status | Owner | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TM-01 | Group listener (spare SIM, linked device, read-only) + searchable inbox API + `inbox.py` + Claude Code skill + deploy workflow | M | Later | — | done | Claude (session 14 Oct, self-improving) | Approved 14 Oct: spare SIM as linked device (accepted WhatsApp-terms risk; Mesiri's business number never used), folder in this repo. **Built 14 Oct (`3a45cff`)**: `teammate/` (listener, `inbox.py`, deploy files, README), skill `.claude/skills/whatsapp-inbox`, manual workflow *Deploy Teammate listener*. Tested: 7 node tests, CLI end-to-end against a running listener, live connection to WhatsApp reached (not linked). **14 Oct switch-on:** secret added, workflow run (listener live, `waiting_for_pairing`). Pairing reached WhatsApp but the session was lost on the post-link restart (state went `logged_out`); fix 1 (`a35057e`): wait for the session to be saved, current WhatsApp client version, log every disconnect reason. Logs then showed the real cause: an unlinked connection dies after ~2.5 min (`408 QR refs attempts ended`), killing the code just issued on it; fix 2: each pair request opens a fresh connection and the link window is ~12 min. **Still waiting:** pair the spare number, add it to groups and label them. **Live 14 Oct:** spare number linked (Baileys 7), listening to the Mercon and Mesiri groups; groups labelled |

Delivered as TM-03a (tagging, approvals from the group) + TM-03b (Claude Code brain, hybrid cloud routines) |

| TM-79 | **What each request used of Claude.** Every bot turn's cost (Claude's own dollar count) and tokens go on the request it was for (the plan it builds, the request it reviews / re-checks, or the one it logged): listener table `item_usage`, `POST /items/:id/usage`, summed on every item (`usage_usd`, `usage_5h`, `usage_7d`, `usage_turns`, `usage_accounts`). | WA-150 | **Request 203, live re-test (2 Nov 21:45): a v3 line that cannot be saved no longer rides along for hours.** Root cause: a held "10 TMT steel bars" kept a `unit_id` the code could not settle; every Save refused it with no buttons and it stayed on every new card.
